Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The ultrasound operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 572. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 238 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 572 + O(1/n^238). Summing over all modes and applying the ultrasound convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
